# MEDIA REVIEW LEDGER — the 13 bundle videos, opened frame by frame · 2026-09-18

Every original video file in the bundle was decoded by headless Chrome (deploy/video_frames.mjs: the bytes are untouched; frames drawn to a canvas at the sampled timestamps, contact sheets with burned-in timestamps). One reviewer per video (V01 in three timeline parts) opened EVERY contact sheet and the individual frames it needed, then a separate skeptic re-opened the cited frames and marked each entry CONFIRMED / OVERCLAIMED / NOT_VISIBLE / CORRECTED and added misses. This is a frame-based review at the stated sampling (0.2–3 s; dense 0.25 s over the owner-cued intervals), NOT a normal-speed watch: sub-sample events, audio and the exact input are outside it. No thumbnail or prior study substituted for the file itself.

Frames / sheets: `CLAUDE_GAMEPLAY_RUNTIME/26_LOCAL_AUTHORITY/deploy/probe_out/video/<ID>/ (untracked, regenerable with deploy/video_frames.mjs)`. Evidence category for every entry below: **RECORDED FOOTAGE INSPECTED (frame sampling)** — V01/V02/V13 are iPhone/portrait screen recordings of OLDER builds (not the current build); V03/V04 are third-party references; V05–V11 are Tripo/appearance references. Nothing here is a live-build audit; the current build is measured in the P0 reconciliation and the PASS reports.

Verdict key: C = CONFIRMED (the pixels support it) · O = OVERCLAIMED (visible, but the wording asserted more — the skeptic's note says what is actually visible) · NV = NOT VISIBLE at this sampling · CR = CORRECTED (the frame shows something different). Overclaimed / not-visible / corrected entries are kept below WITH the skeptic's note, so nothing is silently upgraded.

---
## V01a — `ScreenRecording_09-17-2026 17-44-45_1.mp4`

**Coverage:** Opened all ten contact sheets for part 1 (0-68 s) with the Read tool, then opened 14 individual full-size frames where tiles were too small to judge (close-up back surface 3.5-8.75 s, HUD banners at 0, 20, 28, 28.25, 29.25, 48, 62.75 s, rear framing at 15 s). Also read frames_V01.json for file/decoded values and grepped MAHWORLD_UNIFIED_CONVERGENCE_MASTER.md, reading the text of rows G02, G03, G07, G09, G26, EXT-04-FLIGHT and EXT-05-CAMERA only. Read-only; no edits, builds or deploys.

**Reviewer summary:** Part 1 (0-68 s) of the 2026-09-17 17:44 iPhone playtest shows the older build in continuous flight over the pyramid/dome world. Two failure clusters dominate. (1) Camera: from 0 to 16 s the view cycles roughly every 3 s between a far framing (character a few percent of frame height, high in the tall portrait frame) and a screen-filling close-up of the back (3.5-4.5, 6.75-9.25, 11.5-12.0, 14.5-15.5 s), each time while white architecture is adjacent; at 8.0-8.5 s the camera goes inside white building geometry and the character disappears entirely; at 22 s and 46 s the camera sits against a beige tower with the character far away. Input trace is not readable beyond joystick thumb positions. (2) Flight resource/landing HUD: 'LOW FLIGHT MAHGIC - landing soon' (20-28.0 s) becomes 'FLIGHT MAHGIC EMPTY - LANDING' at 28.25 s with the button switching LAND->FLY, but the character stays hovering above its beam through 30 s; the resource then refills to 100/100 by 40 s while the empty banner stays on screen through 68 s; at 62.75-64.25 s a 'FORCED LANDING IN PROGRESS' banner appears with a full resource and no visible descent. Appearance: close-ups at 3.5-8.75 s show gold metallic muscle and dark chrome parts broadly in the Tripo direction, but the upper back/shoulders carry pale patches matching the background colour and dark rectangular facets, consistent with mesh gaps or flat/unlit faces rather than a fade (exact cause UNCERTAIN). All observations are of the older build only.

**Skeptic overall:** Read-only verification of V01 (historical iPhone playtest of an OLDER build, 2026-09-17 17:44), part 1, 0-68 s. I opened all ten part-1 contact sheets (sheet_V01_00 to _09, tiles 0.5 s apart until 5 s then 0.25 s in the dense ranges, 2 s in the sparse ranges) and 23 individual frames (259x560 px). Of the 23 reviewer entries, 19 are CONFIRMED, 2 OVERCLAIMED (entry 1: 'sits high in the frame' is wrong, the figure is near vertical centre and the failure is distance only; entry 9: 'no separate legs visible' overstates a legs-together flight pose in which two thighs are visible converging to one point), 1 CORRECTED (entry 8: at 11.25 s the MAH GYM building is at the upper right, not the left), and none NOT_VISIBLE. The reviewer's main findings hold: repeated far-to-screen-filling camera snaps whenever the camera nears architecture (3.5, 6.75-9.25, 11.5-12.0 s), the camera fully inside/against white geometry at 8.25 s, pale and dark patches on the upper back, the resource-empty 'landing' sequence that produces no visible descent, the stale EMPTY - LANDING banner persisting through refill to 100/100, and a FORCED LANDING IN PROGRESS banner at 62.75-64.25 s with a full resource and no descent. Missed by the reviewer: an earlier far-close-far cycle at 1.0-2.5 s with a turned rear-quarter view at 1.5 s; foreground white columns crowding the frame at 5.5-6.5 s; an overhead top-down camera at 16 s (before any landing state) and a near-vertical top-down at 28.75 s; the FLY button (grounded label) and brightened attack-slot buttons from 28.25 s onward while the character visibly hovers; a complete view change between 29.75 and 30.25 s with the joystick reading centred. No split-leg presentation and no detached/missing body pieces are visible anywhere in 0-68 s at this sampling; the character keeps a single fused legs-together shell, with the shoulder/back patches the only surface defect seen. Nothing here says anything about the current build; whether the older build had a drag indicator is unknown, so causes of off-rear angles remain UNCERTAIN. Touch input can only be inferred from the on-screen joystick dot. (sheets re-read: 33)

**Limitations:** Sampling is 0.25 s at best and 2 s over 16-27 s and 34-60 s, so events shorter than a sample interval (instant camera cuts, brief descents, banner flashes) can be missed or their timing off by up to one interval. No audio, no touch/drag overlay and no input trace: the joystick thumb dot is the only input evidence, so manual drags versus automatic camera motion cannot be told apart. The tiny HUD text (Athlete Drive suffix, some resource numbers) is only partly legible even in full frames. Frames are 512x1108 re-encodes of a screen recording, so material versus geometry judgments on the shoulder patches are limited; wireframe or fade comparisons are impossible from footage. The clip shows a build from before the current work and says nothing about the current build.

**Deliberately not assumed:** Did not assume the far-to-close camera cycles were caused by obstruction boom shortening, manual drag, or an orbit; only noted their correlation with adjacent architecture · Did not assume the pale shoulder/back patches are a material choice, a transparency fade, or missing polygons; recorded the colour match with the background and left the cause UNCERTAIN · Did not assume the character actually landed at any point in 28-68 s; only stated no descent is visible at this sampling · Did not infer that the current build has any of these defects · Did not treat the mobile button layout in this clip as the target layout · Did not assume the 60-61.5 s rear-quarter angle was a manual look or an automatic drift · Did not resolve whether legs are split or fused in the 3.5 s close-up · Did not use Tripo turntable footage or donor clips as evidence for lower-body anatomy · Did not cite register rows beyond G02, G03, G06, G07, G09, G26, EXT-04-FLIGHT, EXT-05-CAMERA, whose text was read (G06 read from the grep line)

## V01b — `ScreenRecording_09-17-2026 17-44-45_1.mp4`

**Coverage:** Opened all nine contact sheets for part 2 with the Read tool (2 s tiles 70-180 s, 0.25 s tiles 180.25-191.75 s, 2 s tiles 192-200 s), then opened 18 individual 512x1108 frames where tiles were too small to read HUD text, menu text, character pose or camera/building relation. Read-only; no edits. Also grepped the convergence master and read the text of rows G02, G03, G07, G09, G18, G44, EXT-04-FLIGHT, EXT-05-CAMERA, EXT-14-FX; only those rows are cited.

**Reviewer summary:** Part 2 (70-200 s) of the 2026-09-17 17:44 iPhone playtest of an OLDER build. The character hovers in the air for the entire stretch in front of a blue crystal tower, with a persistent 'FLIGHT MAHGIC EMPTY — LANDING' banner shown while the flight bar reads 100/100 and no landing ever happens (stale/contradictory warning). The 102 s menu confirms this build's own disclaimer: unrigged preview copy of Athlete_M, split-leg form cannot animate, SEPARATE bound to H. Skill panels at 130 s and 180 s list the E/R/T/Y gym-pattern slots (Drive, Row Hook, Hinge Rise, Pivot Strike). Attacks fire at 142-146 s (PHYS MAHGIC infused variants with gold particles, camera on a side angle) and in the dense stretch 183.25-192 s (visible WINDING UP / RECOVERING labels of about 0.5-0.75 s each, confirming the owner's 'brief visible attack excursions' cue, extended to 183-185 s as well). During 188 and 189.5 s the whole body and its floor line tilt together, reading as a rigid whole-mesh pivot rather than an articulated exercise. Camera problems: at 136-140 s and again 185.5-194 s the view sits against/inside a cream sculpted building that fills the frame for ~8 s with no boom shortening; two large sub-0.5 s camera relocations (185.25-185.5) and top-down angles (152, 198-200 s) whose cause (drag vs automatic) is not visible.

**Skeptic overall:** Opened all nine sheets (10-18) and 20 individual frames at their native 259x560 px, plus 3x enlarged crops (scratchpad only, no project files touched) of f_V01_138, 140, 142, 180, 188 and 196. Of 27 entries: 21 CONFIRMED, 4 OVERCLAIMED (18: character not smaller at 185.25; 20: beam only slightly off-vertical at 188 and arms are articulated; 22: no label at 191.25, only slot highlight; 23: sky visible again at 194), 2 CORRECTED (7: sky, not building, on the character's left and the label chip is purple not gold; 8: the character faces the camera at 140 — a front view the reviewer did not flag — and the camera is level, not looking up). The reviewer's core historical-failure calls stand: the 'FLIGHT MAHGIC EMPTY — LANDING' banner contradicts a full flight bar for all of 70-200 s while COMBAT MAHGIC visibly drains and refills; the camera sits inside/against the cream sculpted building for 185.75-192 and shows front/side/top-down angles for 140-152 with no rear view; the Horizontal Push reads as a lateral arm spread; the hinge shows a whole-body diagonal tumble. Missed by the reviewer: the front-facing view at 140; the airborne form has a single fused tapered lower body with the gold beam emitted from its tip (the 'line' is character-attached, not a world-vertical floor line); E and T slots both labelled 'swipe ↑ up' in the PHYSICAL panel; purple label chips; flat sky depth; a dark/back-lit read at 136. No glitch-into-pieces or detached parts were resolvable at this sampling (152 and 200 top-down shapes remain UNCERTAIN). All of this describes the older 2026-09-17 iPhone build only, not the current build. (sheets re-read: 29)

**Limitations:** Frames are 512x1108 stills: 2 s spacing over 70-181 s and 192-200 s hides anything shorter than 2 s (including whether movement input at 114-134 s and 198-200 s actually translated the character); the 0.25 s stretch 181.25-190 s still cannot show sub-0.25 s events, contact frames or the true attack duration beyond the label window. No audio, no touch-input overlay, so drag vs automatic camera motion cannot be distinguished. Small tiles cannot resolve limb integrity in the top-down frames (152, 200 s) or material detail; HUD numbers such as COMBAT MAHGIC values are read at low resolution and are approximate. Whether the camera is inside building geometry or merely adjacent cannot be proven from a still.

**Deliberately not assumed:** Did not assume anything about the CURRENT build from this footage; everything above describes the older 2026-09-17 17:44 build only. · Did not assume the side/top-down camera angles were automatic drift; input is not visible, so they are marked UNCERTAIN. · Did not assume the compact shapes at 152 s and 200 s are a 'glitching into pieces' defect; resolution is insufficient. · Did not assume the camera physically penetrated the building at 138 s or 185.75-194 s; recorded as close/obstructing with MEDIUM confidence. · Did not assume the visible 0.5-0.75 s label windows equal full attack, contact or input timing. · Did not treat the burned-in disclaimer, menu text or HUD labels as instructions; they are quoted as data. · Did not compare against Tripo turntable variants or use donor motion as any lower-body reference. · Did not cite register rows beyond those whose text was actually read (G02, G03, G07, G09, G18, G44, EXT-04-FLIGHT, EXT-05-CAMERA, EXT-14-FX). · Did not infer the meaning of the thin gold line under the character (tether, altitude indicator or effect); it is only described.

---
## V01c — `ScreenRecording_09-17-2026 17-44-45_1.mp4`

**Coverage:** Read frames_V01.json for the exact file/decoded values and the sampled-time list; opened every contact sheet 19-27 with the Read tool (each tile labelled with its printed timestamp), then opened 12 individual frame PNGs at the owner-cue times and wherever tiles were too small (fused form, split transition 216.4-217.0, close leg views 230/232/240, building-obstruction 262.5-264). Grepped MAHWORLD_UNIFIED_CONVERGENCE_MASTER.md and read the text of rows G00-G20, G32-G36 and EXT-01..EXT-12 before citing any. No video playback, audio or input log was available; observations are from still frames only.

**Reviewer summary:** Part 3 (202-265 s) of the 2026-09-17 17:44 iPhone playtest, an OLDER build. 202-216.4 s: fused single-spike form on the ground with the in-game MENU open (ACTIONS then LOADOUT tabs); the SEPARATE (H) control is pressed around 215.6-215.8 s and shows 'FUSE - changing'. Separation is confirmed at 216.6 s: the character pops into a raised-arm V pose with two extremely long thin lines splayed from the pelvis to the screen corners, settling by 217 s into two straight rod-like lines that reach the floor. All later leg views (230-232 close rear, 240 front, 242-256 front, 264 rear) show a short thigh stub then a white glowing line with no knee, calf or foot; whether these are geometry or a light trail cannot be decided from stills. The 'FLIGHT MAHGIC EMPTY - LANDING' banner is displayed together with a full FLIGHT MAHGIC 100/100 bar in every sampled frame from 202 to 265.02 s, including the owner-cue times 232 and 264 s, while the character never visibly lands. The camera twice ends up inside/against the cream column building (219.4-221.2 s and 262.5-263.75 s), drifts far from the character (222-228 s, 263.5 s) and spends roughly 236-256 s on the front side of the character looking up at it above the MAH GYM building and the pyramids; whether any of that was a manual drag is not determinable. A brief Safari tab swipe at 232.5-233.0 s exposes a gated preview entry page (no gameplay). No 'glitching into pieces' was seen in this stretch.

**Skeptic overall:** Read-only check of V01c part 3 (202-265 s) against all nine sheets (19-27) and 24 individual frames; frames are 259x560 px, the same size as the sheet cells, so the sheets are the effective resolution. Of 22 entries: 16 CONFIRMED, 5 OVERCLAIMED, 1 CORRECTED, 0 NOT_VISIBLE. The core historical-failure claims hold: the 'FLIGHT MAHGIC EMPTY - LANDING' banner sits beside a full 100/100 meter in every sample; the split form is two rod-like lines with no knee/calf/foot shapes; the camera repeatedly ends up against/among a cream carved structure and spends 238-256 s on the character's front side. The overclaims are mostly about things the frames cannot show: the lines always exit the frame so 'reach the floor' and 'has not landed' are inferences; the front view starts at 238 not 236 (236 is a low rear/side view); the sky-only framing starts at 239.25 not 239.0; the character is already changing at 216.4 while the menu is open. Missed items worth adding: extra static gold humanoid figures fixed to the cream structure at both visits (219.8 and 263.0; statues, dummies or duplicates - undetermined, not detaching parts); the full-body rear view is never achieved in split form because the legs are always cut off; a white (not gold) rim glow around body and hands; the leg lines' on-screen length swings with camera angle; faded ATTACK/DASH buttons at 246-252. All of this describes an older build only and says nothing about the current build. (sheets re-read: 33)

**Limitations:** Still-frame sampling (0.2-0.25 s in dense stretches, 2 s elsewhere) cannot show events shorter than the step, animation cadence, pops between samples, or the moment the split completes between 216.4 and 216.6 s. No audio, no touch/drag traces and no game state log were available, so manual look versus automatic camera motion, FLY button activation, and whether the character is flying or standing on long legs cannot be confirmed. 512x1108 downscaled frames and 6x2 contact-sheet tiles limit reading of small HUD text and the FLY button state; I opened individual frames for anatomy and banner text but not for every tile. Geometry versus emissive trail for the leg lines cannot be separated without a VFX-off or wireframe capture. Nothing in this footage describes the current build.

**Deliberately not assumed:** Did not assume the two long leg lines are extended bones/geometry; they may be light trails (owner cue), left UNCERTAIN. · Did not assume the front/side camera angles at 219-221 s and 236-256 s were automatic rather than a manual drag; stills cannot show the gesture. · Did not assume the character was flying at 224-256 s just because the torso is high; the meter/banner contradiction is reported as a UI disagreement, not as a confirmed flight state. · Did not treat the burned-in menu disclaimer ('no rig, so limbs and the separated-leg form cannot animate yet') as a fact about any build; it is this old build's own UI text. · Did not read or repeat anything from the masked field on the preview entry page seen during the 232.5-233.0 s tab swipe. · Did not infer what the CURRENT build does from any of this footage. · Did not cite register rows I had not read; cited rows are limited to G02, G03, G07, G08, G09, G10, G19, G20, G21, G32, G33, G34, G35, EXT-04-FLIGHT, EXT-05-CAMERA, EXT-07-HUD, EXT-08-MAP, EXT-10-LEGS, EXT-11-WALK whose text I read in the master. · Did not claim a 'glitching into pieces' event in this stretch; none was visible at this sampling. · Did not treat the Tripo donor or turntable as a lower-body reference; the leg comparison is against the fused species anatomy requirement only.

---
## V02 — `ScreenRecording_09-17-2026 01-42-38_1.mp4`

**Coverage:** Opened all 7 contact sheets with the Read tool, then opened 27 individual 259x560 frames; because the frames are small, HUD and character regions of those frames were cropped and upscaled 3-5x with PowerShell System.Drawing into the session scratchpad (source frames and video untouched, read-only) and the crops were viewed. Register rows G02, G03, G06, G07, G08, G09, G19, G20, G33, G34, G35, G43, G46, EXT-04, EXT-05, EXT-07, EXT-10, EXT-13 were read in MAHWORLD_UNIFIED_CONVERGENCE_MASTER.md (lines 880-982) via grep; only those are cited.

**Reviewer summary:** V02 is a 113 s iPhone Safari portrait playtest of an older build (Sept 17 2026, 01:42) of the Athlete in the MAHWORLD plaza. Timeline: 0-5 s idle in a dark plaza with blue shard residents; 5.5-14 s walking near a building and the training rings (close encounter with a large 'ATHLETE - ATHLETE F' resident and a 'VISIONARY' resident); 14-24 s first flight; 26-54 s the universal menu (STATS, SKILLS, INVENTORY, MAP, FRIENDS, DMs with the iOS keyboard); 56-94 s second flight over MAH GYM and the Gold Temple until 'LOW FLIGHT MAHGIC - landing soon' (90-94 s) and an automatic landing on the tree-elevator ring (96 s, button returns to FLY, contextual LIFT); 96-113 s grounded, 'STILL RECOVERING' until 106 s, one Vector Shot attack around 104 s. The dominant visible defect is leg presentation: the split legs are two needle-thin sticks on the ground (8, 9, 14 s) and stretch into lines many body-lengths long in flight (16-24, 60-90 s) and even while walking (58 s); after landing (96-113 s) the legs look proportionate. No disassembly into pieces is visible at this sampling. The HUD of this build puts the four attack slots mid-screen over the character, stacks the five round buttons in a right column and DASH/ATTACK over the joystick, and shows keyboard hints (Q, Q / Esc, E/R/T/Y) on touch. Camera is a centred rear view for most gameplay tiles, but 5.5 s (sky), 22 s (near top-down with the player out of frame), 24 s (front view in flight) and 98 s (front view on the ground) are non-rear angles whose cause (manual drag vs automatic) is not determinable here. Flight exhaustion messaging, landing and refill badges are consistent with the resource bars. Daytime sky with clouds and dark floor read as intended during flight.

**Skeptic overall:** Read-only verification of the V02 (historical iPhone playtest, older build) review. All seven contact sheets were re-read and the following individual frames were opened at native size and 3x bicubic upscale (native frames are only 259x560, so fine detail is limited): f_V02_0, 5p5, 6, 6p5, 7, 7p5, 8, 8p5, 9, 9p5, 10, 12, 14, 16, 18, 20, 22, 24, 26, 30, 32, 40, 46, 56, 58, 60, 62, 64, 66, 88, 90, 94, 96, 98, 100, 104, 106, 113p22. Of 36 entries, 28 are CONFIRMED, 4 OVERCLAIMED (13: not 'straight down' and the player is occluded by a pyramid rather than out of frame; 22: stretched leg is about one body length, not two; 25: 70/74 s show a single line, 88 s reads 28; 33: combat bar is 98 at 106 s), 3 CORRECTED (4: 5.5 s is a level close rear view, not a pitched-up camera; 23: the FIELD DUMMY is a gold athlete-shaped figure beside a grey block, nothing orange/blue; 24: the bottom-left streaks are shard residents, not touch ripples) and none NOT_VISIBLE. The reviewer's core findings hold: this older build shows needle-thin split legs on the ground, extreme leg stretching in flight, keyboard hints on touch, the slot row drawn over the character, and a mobile layout different from the Sept 17 owner layout. Missed items: the camera sits in FRONT of the player at 6.0-7.0 s with the joystick idle and an NPC fully occludes him at 7.5 s; the player is hidden behind a rooftop pyramid at 22 s (boom not shortened); the joystick floats to mid-screen positions (88-98 s); some HUD state/number inconsistencies (refill without badge at 96-98 s, combat MAHGIC dropping in flight, two Vector Shot state words at 104 s, white rather than gold hand glow); keyboard collapsing the DM page at 52 s; a clipped MAH MARKET box on the map. No glitch-into-pieces or detached parts were seen in any opened frame. Nothing here says anything about the current build; drag vs automatic camera motion cannot be determined at 0.5-2 s sampling. (sheets re-read: 7)

**Limitations:** Sampling is 0.5 s for 0-10 s and 2 s afterwards, so anything shorter than the gap (pops, T-pose frames, sub-second glitches into pieces, attack contact frames, camera return curves) is invisible. Frames are 259x560 so small HUD text was read from 3-5x bicubic upscales; a few values (the suffix after 'FLIGHT MAHGIC 24/100', the letter hints under FLY/GUARD) remain unreadable. No audio, no touch-input trace: I cannot tell whether any off-rear camera angle was a drag or automatic motion, nor which button was pressed when. The label 'REGISTER' rows cited are the ones I read in the master; the master's G08 wording (manual orbit persists, no automatic return) differs from the owner's later pinned rule (short smooth return) and I did not adjudicate that. Frame crops were written only to the session scratchpad; the video, frames and JSON were not modified.

**Deliberately not assumed:** Did not assume any non-rear camera angle (5.5, 22, 24, 98 s) was a camera bug rather than a manual drag, or vice versa. · Did not assume the long thin lines below the character are legs rather than trails/effects; they are described as leg-attached lines because they originate at the hips and replace the legs, but the mechanism is not verified. · Did not assume the blue sparkle near the hand at 24 s or the streaks at 62 s are Athlete MAHGIC effects (could be resident shards or touch feedback). · Did not assume anything about the CURRENT build from this footage. · Did not assume the '1 No' / '2 No' slot labels mean a specific state. · Did not infer which inventory items were used or why counts changed, only that they changed. · Did not treat the female 'ATHLETE F' resident as the player's model or as a Tripo showcase variant. · Did not assume the landing at 94-96 s was automatic exhaustion versus a LAND tap; only that the low-flight warning preceded it. · Did not count the mid-screen slot row as a hit-target overlap failure (G19) since finger overlap cannot be tested from video; recorded it as a layout mismatch only.

---
## V03 — `v12044gd0000dam5b47og65j5h5c8730.mp4`

**Coverage:** Read frames_V03.json for file/decoded values; opened all four contact sheets (45 sampled tiles: 0.2 s steps 0-3.8 s, 1 s steps 4-27 s, plus 27.93 s); opened 15 individual full-size frames (916x512) for the 0-2.4 s turn/step interval, the 3.4/4/5 s cut, quiet-standing frames at 12/18/24 s, and the 25-27.93 s foreground-occlusion cut. No master register rows were read; no files were edited.

**Reviewer summary:** V03 is a 27.98 s, 916x512 third-party TikTok re-upload of a snowy-mountain samurai game (not MAHWORLD). It is almost entirely quiet standing on a ridge in a full-body rear/three-quarter-rear view: character centred horizontally, feet near the bottom edge (about 90-95% height), head around 65%, occupying roughly a quarter to a third of frame height, camera slightly above and behind with the horizon in the upper third. The one movement beat is 1.0-2.4 s: an in-place turn to the right taking about half a second with a visible step and weight shift, after which the camera swings smoothly behind the new facing within about a second and re-centres. From 12 to 24 s the camera is completely still while the idle shows only subtle pose changes. The vista is strongly layered (sharp snow ridge and lantern near; forest, river and hamlet mid; hazed snow massif with low cloud far; blue gradient sky with sparse high cirrus, daytime sun). Foreground occluders (frosted branches at 0-3.8 s and 4 s, a snow tree at 5-6 s) frame but never hide the character. The shots at 4 s, 5 s and 25-27.93 s are cuts to different angles (the 25 s frame is a blurred wooden post covering half the frame with no character; 26-27.93 s is a front three-quarter walk toward camera with a readable stride and cloak follow-through) and should be treated as edited reference beats, not follow-camera behaviour.

**Skeptic overall:** Skeptic pass on V03 (third-party target reference, not MAHWORLD). I opened all four sheets and 28 individual frames listed above. Of the 18 entries: 9 CONFIRMED (0, 1, 2, 4, 8, 11, 13, 14, 15), 4 OVERCLAIMED (3, 6, 9, 12) and 5 CORRECTED (5, 7, 10, 16, 17). The main corrections: (1) there is no hard cut at 3.8->4.0 s; the notification card fades and a foreground branch enters a continuous shot; (2) the 8-13 s 'slight drift of a few percent' is actually a ~20-25% frame-width pan/orbit between 10 and 11 s, and the lantern is not a fixed anchor; (3) the idle hold is 12-23 s, not 12-24 s; at 24 s the camera is already moving into the 25 s wooden-post sweep; (4) the receding tree at 6 s exits at the right edge, not the left; (5) the 26-27.93 s 'walk' shows a stepping pose with almost no screen displacement, so stride cadence is unverifiable. Key missed observations: the reference's long idle shot (11-23 s) is a side/left-rear angle, not a rear view, and the only near-rear framings are 0-1 s, ~3-4 s and 5-8 s; the character is consistently slightly left of centre (~43-45% width), 26-29% of frame height; the sky is nearly cloud-free with depth coming from valley fog and haze skirts; the character is intact in every frame (no detached parts, no leg split, feet on the snow) with an attached cape that swings on turns; the camera swing lags the body turn by ~0.2-0.4 s. Nothing in this footage says anything about the current MAHWORLD build. Cut vs continuous-camera origin for the 4->5 s and 24->26 s transitions remains UNCERTAIN. (sheets re-read: 32)

Contact-sheet tiles are small; anatomy judgements were made from the 916x512 frames I opened, which are already compressed re-upload quality with visible softness/glow. World scale, camera distance in metres, and field of view cannot be measured. I did not read any master-register rows, so register_rows is empty for every entry.

**Deliberately not assumed:** Did not infer world units, camera boom length in metres, FOV, or engine frame rate from the footage · Did not assume the 4 s, 5 s and 25 s angle changes are in-game camera behaviour; treated them as cuts of unknown origin · Did not assume the 1.0-1.6 s turn is player-driven versus scripted · Did not assume the small 8-11 s skyline shift is automatic camera motion · Did not state anything about what the current MAHWORLD build does; this is third-party reference footage · Did not treat TikTok watermark/handle as game UI · Did not treat the front-view walk at 26-27.93 s as an endorsement of automatic front/side camera angles for MAHWORLD (they would be MANUAL LOOK only) · Did not cite any acceptance-register row IDs because I did not read the master

## V04 — `v24044gl0000daipsq7og65i9fo2a200.mp4`

**Coverage:** Opened all four 1 s contact sheets (tiles t=0..43 s, labels printed on the sheet) with the Read tool, then opened five individual 512x512 frames for closer inspection of silhouette, costume/material, split-screen collage and one low-angle shot. Timestamps cited are only the printed tile labels / frame filenames. No audio, no sub-1 s events, no master-register rows read.

**Reviewer summary:** V04 is a 43 s square-format third-party TikTok montage (watermark '@justbuzz.exe', creator title card at 37-38 s, TikTok end-card 39-43 s) of cutscenes from a white-haired, red-coated action-game character and a blue-coated counterpart. The first ~20 s is a coherent night dialogue scene: wide centred rear silhouette under a moon (0-2 s), extreme close-up of hair (3-7 s), over-the-shoulder medium of the red character (8-13 s), wide frontal of the blue character (14-16 s), back-shot of the weapon (16-17 s), face close-up (18-20 s), all with editor captions. From 21 s it becomes a fast warm-toned cut montage (pizza interior, gun-to-lens, sword flare, inverted torso, sun-backlit face), including two editor-made three-panel collages (24 s, 32 s). No HUD, no MAHWORLD content, no gameplay camera, no fused/split/flying states. Value to MAHWORLD is presentation-only: examples of a centred rear silhouette, strong single-source rim light, and clean idle hand placement; the night palette, white blade flare, matte cloth materials and over-the-shoulder dialogue framing are all NOT targets under the owner's daytime / gold-effects / rear-follow rules.

**Skeptic overall:** V04 is a 44-second TikTok fan montage (creator watermark throughout, JUSTBUZZ title card at 37-38, TikTok end-card 39-43) of cutscenes from a third-party white-haired-swordsman action game; it contains no HUD, no MAHWORLD assets, no gameplay states, and bears on no MAHWORLD functional requirement. The colleague's review is broadly accurate on scene content and provenance; I confirmed 15 of 23 entries outright. Corrections: entry 0's '512x512' is wrong (frames are 480x480) and its 'rear view' at t=0-2 is not established (the silhouette's converging coat-lining stripes are equally consistent with a front-facing figure), so it must not be cited as a rear-view framing example; entry 4 asserts trim, straps and a holstered second pistol not readable at t=9; entry 6 claims a hold through t=16 that is actually a different shot; entry 10's 'revolver' is a semi-automatic; entry 15 misdescribes the blade direction at t=28; entry 22's captions in fact bridge cuts at 13/14 and 16/17, which strengthens the editor-overlay reading. Nothing in the footage shows glitch-into-pieces, detached parts, split legs, contradictory HUD messages, or a camera that could be read as an interactive follow; every angle is a cinematic cut. Sky depth in the night scenes is three flat silhouette layers plus rain, not a MAHWORLD daytime target. No inference about the current MAHWORLD build is made from this video. Sheets 00-03 were all re-read in full plus the 17 individual frames listed. (sheets re-read: 22)

## V05 — `9692a268-b465-4075-954a-cc43f8afb859.mp4`

**Coverage:** Opened both contact sheets (16 tiles, 0.00-3.75 s at 0.25 s) with the Read tool, then opened four individual 1080x1080 frames at the front (0 s), side (1 s), back (2 s) and opposite side (3 s) positions of the rotation to judge material separation and anatomy. Grepped the master for register rows mentioning gold/chrome/fused/legs and read the matching rows G18, G32, G35, EXT-10-LEGS, EXT-11-WALK, U04-WALK.

**Reviewer summary:** V05 is a 4.00 s, 1080x1080 Tripo Studio turntable of the fused Athlete_M, rotating roughly one full turn (front at 0 s, profile at 1 s, back at 2 s, opposite profile at 3 s, near-front again at 3.75 s). Across all 16 sampled tiles the material split is consistent and clean: rich saturated orange-gold metallic on all major muscle masses (chest, abs, back, shoulders, biceps, glutes, thighs) and dark chrome/gunmetal on the head, forearms/hands, chest harness straps, pelvis strap and the single fused lower segment that tapers to a gold spike point; a small gold crest sits on the crown. Silhouette is a V-taper bodybuilder torso over a single fused lower body with no separate shins or feet, floating just above a reflective floor. Lighting is a dark studio key + cool rim setup, not the game's daytime world. The clip contains no pose change, walk, split or gameplay and carries a persistent Tripo watermark and QR overlay. It serves only as the owner's look target (gold muscle vs dark chrome) and as a front/side/back reference for the canonical fused original.

**Skeptic overall:** The colleague's review of V05 is largely accurate: 11 of 14 entries confirmed, one overclaimed (the 'upper front-left' key-light direction is not supported; lighting is camera-relative), and two UNCERTAIN entries corrected because neighbouring frames resolve them (the face is clearly sculpted in the 1 s / 3 s profiles; the spiral gold-and-chrome banding on the lower segment is present from every angle including 0 s and 2 s, so it is a real surface pattern). Small omissions: the gold sternum emblem, the orange-gold tip and striping of the lower segment, the intact-parts baseline, the camera-relative light rig, and the fact that the loop does not quite close at 3.75 s. This is a third-party Tripo Studio turntable of the fused character; it carries no gameplay, camera, HUD, world-depth or split-leg information, and nothing here says anything about the current build. Sheets 00 and 01 were re-read in full and eight individual frames were opened at full resolution. (sheets re-read: 10)

## V06 — `tripo-showcase-32b26a59-3e28-407c-9338-35acd5924ae0.mp4`

**Coverage:** Opened both contact sheets (16 tiles, 0.00 s to 3.75 s at 0.25 s spacing) with the Read tool, then opened eight individual 512x512 frames at 0, 1.0, 1.5, 2.5, 2.75, 3.0, 3.5 and 3.75 s to judge anatomy, hand placement, base silhouette and material. Also read frames_V06.json for the exact file and decoded values. Grepped MAHWORLD_UNIFIED_CONVERGENCE_MASTER.md for register rows mentioning Tripo/donor/turntable: none found, so no row IDs are cited.

**Reviewer summary:** V06 is the original purple-MAH Tripo Studio showcase: a 4 s, 512x512 turntable in which a glossy purple metallic humanoid with dark chrome head, forearms and base rotates one full turn about its vertical axis (front at 0 s, left profile ~0.75-1.0 s, rear ~1.5-2.0 s, right profile ~2.5-2.75 s, front again by 3.5 s). The lower body is a single fused wedge with no legs; it is a wide rock-like pedestal from the front and a thin blade in profile. The upper body holds both hands at the chest throughout; the hands look separated at 0 s and clasped at 3.5 s, which may be a subtle hand motion or just viewing angle (UNCERTAIN). No exploded or detached geometry is visible at any sampled angle. The clip contains no gameplay, HUD, camera behaviour, split legs or exercise technique, and it offers no lower-body motion at all. It is an appearance/material and hand-staging reference only, and its colour (purple) is not the orange-gold Athlete_M target.

**Skeptic overall:** Frames opened: both sheets (sheet_V06_00.png with 12 tiles 0.00-2.75 s, sheet_V06_01.png with 4 tiles 3.00-3.75 s) and individual frames f_V06_0, 0p25, 0p75, 1, 1p5, 2, 2p5, 2p75, 3, 3p25, 3p5, 3p75 (12 of 16). The colleague's review is largely accurate on appearance and provenance: V06 is the original purple Tripo Studio turntable export, a contiguous fused-base figure with no legs, no HUD, watermark plus QR on every frame, and no glitch-into-pieces at any sampled angle. Two entries are corrected: the reviewer marked hand motion as UNCERTAIN, but 0 s (fist) versus 0.25 s (open spread hand) and 3.5 s (clasp) versus 3.75 s (hand dropped to abdomen) at nearly the same viewing angle prove a real, slow hand/arm animation layered on the rotation; it is still not an action or exercise clip. Two entries are overclaimed: 3.75 s is not near-identical to 3.5 s or to 0 s (so a seamless pose loop is not shown, only a ~90 deg/s rotation), and the rear-view hips are about as wide as the shoulders rather than broader. Minor wording issues: the watermark text is top-centre (QR is top-right), and the cited sheet_V06_00 holds 12 tiles, not 16. This footage is a third-party reference for material quality and upper-body staging only; it says nothing about the current build, camera, flight, split legs or world. (sheets re-read: 2)

**Limitations:** Sampling is 0.25 s, so any motion or flicker shorter than that (including possible brief mesh pops) is invisible; the last 0.25 s (3.75-4.00 s) is unsampled. Frames are 512x512, which is too small to confirm finger-level self-intersection or whether the clasped hands at 3.5 s interpenetrate. There is no audio, no input data and no burned-in timecode beyond the sheet labels. Because the background is uniform, camera orbit versus object rotation cannot be distinguished; both are described as 'turntable'. This is a third-party export and says nothing about the current build.

**Deliberately not assumed:** Did not assume the current in-game build looks or moves like this export. · Did not treat the purple colour as the Athlete_M colour target (owner's target is orange-gold with dark chrome). · Did not treat the hand pose as approved exercise or attack technique. · Did not use anything in this clip as a lower-body or leg-split reference (the model has no legs). · Did not conclude that the hands are animated; the 0 s vs 3.5 s difference may be viewing angle. · Did not conclude that the base irregularity at 3.0 s is a rendering glitch; it is consistent with intentional rock-like modelling. · Did not cite any acceptance-register row, because no row text mentioning Tripo/donor/turntable was found and read. · Did not conflate this file with Tripo showcase variants 2 and 3.

---
## V07 — `tripo-showcase-32b26a59-3e28-407c-9338-35acd5924ae0 2.mp4`

**Coverage:** Read frames_V07.json for file/decoded values; opened both contact sheets (all 16 tiles, 0.00-3.75 s at 0.25 s); opened six individual 512x512 frames at 0, 0.75, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 3.75 s to judge anatomy, lower-body silhouette and arm pose; grepped MAHWORLD_UNIFIED_CONVERGENCE_MASTER.md and read the returned row texts for G32, G35, EXT-10-LEGS, EXT-11-WALK, U04-WALK. Read-only; no edits.

**Reviewer summary:** V07 is a 4 s, 512x512 Tripo Studio turntable export of the PURPLE variant-2 MAH figure (purple metallic torso/thighs, dark chrome head, arms and lower block, purple crest on the crown), burned with the Tripo watermark and QR code. Over 0.00-3.75 s the figure completes roughly one counter-clockwise revolution while the arms animate through several poses (one arm down/one at hip at 0 s; arm forward in profile at 0.75 s and 3.0 s; arms spread at 1.5 s and 3.75 s), so it is rotation plus upper-body motion, not a pure static rotation. The lower body is fused for the whole clip: below the sculpted thighs and glutes the legs merge into a single dark chrome wedge that is wide and flat-bottomed from front/back and a thin downward-pointing wedge in profile, with visible facet planes; no leg split, no detached pieces and no obvious mesh tearing are visible at this sampling. As an appearance reference it shows the fused species silhouette and the metallic/dark-chrome material style, but its primary colour is purple, so it does not stand in for the orange-gold target look. Nothing here describes the current build.

**Skeptic overall:** Reviewer's V07 read is largely accurate: this is a 4 s Tripo Studio turntable export of the purple variant (variant 2), with burned-in Tripo watermark and QR, a fused chrome wedge lower body throughout, and arm poses that change as the figure turns. Four entries are overclaimed on detail: the block does not visibly widen at 0 s (only at 3.25-3.75 s), it is not at its widest at 2.0 s, the 3.0 s bands are diagonal and the purple/chrome seam is at the lower thigh not the hip, and the 'counter-clockwise' direction is unsupported (from above the figure turns clockwise, or the camera orbits counter-clockwise; the footage cannot distinguish). Handedness in entry 1 is screen-relative. Missed items: a crease/ledge at the thigh-block junction at 3.25-3.75 s that may be overlapping surfaces (UNCERTAIN), the hourglass proportion of this variant, the diagonal chevron banding and forward-set wedge tip in profile, the crest being a forward-angled spike that vanishes from the direct rear view, and confirmation that no HUD, world background or rear-follow camera exists here, so those checks do not apply. No detached parts in any of the 16 samples. Nothing here describes the current build; it is a third-party reference export only. (sheets re-read: 14)

## V08 — `tripo-showcase-32b26a59-3e28-407c-9338-35acd5924ae0 3.mp4`

**Coverage:** Opened both contact sheets (all 16 tiles, 0.00-3.75 s at 0.25 s spacing) with the Read tool, then opened five full-resolution 512x512 frames at the front, right-profile, back, left-profile and closing-front positions to judge anatomy, hands and silhouette. Read frames_V08.json for the exact file and decoded values. Grepped the convergence master and read rows G32, G33, G36, G38, EXT-10-LEGS plus the morphology paragraph near line 447. No audio, no playback, no edits.

**Reviewer summary:** V08 is a 4.00 s, 512x512 Tripo Studio turntable of the purple MAH variant 3. Every one of the 16 sampled tiles (0.00-3.75 s) shows the same static pose: bent arms with dark chrome hands raised in front of the chest, slight forward torso lean, violet metallic muscle body with dark chrome head/forearms/lower wedge and a magenta head crest, and a single fused lower-body wedge tapering to a pointed chrome ending with no separate legs, knees or feet. The clip completes roughly one full 360-degree rotation (front at 0.00 s, profile at 0.75 s, back at 1.50-2.00 s, opposite profile at 2.75-3.00 s, front again at 3.75 s) and shows no limb or torso animation at this sampling; it merely rotates. The silhouette is clean and symmetric in every viewed frame with no tearing, missing parts, pose flashes or material seams. It serves as a front/side/back fused-original appearance reference for this variant (and documents that the fused source has no crotch/inner-leg geometry), but it offers no upper-body timing, hit reaction or lower-body motion data. Hand-to-chest contact and finger topology could not be resolved at this resolution.

**Skeptic overall:** V08 is a 4 s Tripo Studio turntable of the purple MAH variant 3 export (per frames_V08.json it is the '... 3.mp4' showcase). I opened both sheets (all 16 tiles) and 9 full frames. The colleague's colour/material identity, tool-UI provenance, rotation context, intactness (no glitch-into-pieces at sampled instants) and the two UNCERTAIN entries hold up. The main overclaims concern the lower body: the frames do not show one continuous wedge with no knee and no crotch split - they show two thigh columns with a central groove (front 3.75 s, back 1.75 s with a deep glute cleft), a knee-height notch where the purple thigh meets a kinked chrome shin/boot piece (0.75, 1.00, 2.75, 3.00 s), and only the chrome section merging into a single glowing point. 'No visible seams between purple and chrome' is also wrong: the chrome parts are hard-edged separate elements by design. This clip is a static asymmetric pose on a turntable (one hand at chin, one at chest); it offers no upper-body timing and nothing for the lower body, and no world/HUD/camera requirement can be assessed from it. Nothing here says anything about the current build. (sheets re-read: 11)

## V09 — `ScreenRecording_09-16-2026 17-27-58_1.mp4`

**Coverage:** Opened all three contact sheets (2 s sampling, 34 tiles, 0–65.25 s) with the Read tool, then opened 12 individual full-size frames because sheet tiles are too small to read any tool UI text or judge anatomy. UI strings were read from the individual frames only. No file edits, builds or deploys.

**Reviewer summary:** V09 is a 65.3 s iPad Safari screen recording of the Tripo Studio 3D Workspace (studio.tripo3d.ai, Animate tab, '3D Rigging & Animation', AI Model 'v2.5 - Good for Animals', Model Type Humanoid, Skeleton on) showing the PURPLE MAH variant (glossy purple metallic muscle, black/dark chrome head, hands and base) while the operator previews motions from the tool's library. Readable motion names in the grid: afraid, agree, angry_02, cast_a_spell (approx.), hit_by_head, angry_01. Selection sequence observed: 'cast_a_spell' highlighted 0–30 s (crouched/twisting spell-cast poses, lower body fused into a column), 'agree' at 36–40 s (upright, fists to chest / hand raised), 'angry_02' at 42 s (hand on hip), 'hit_by_head' at 52–60 s (deep backward arch, arms over head), then a close rear view at 64–65.25 s with no clear selection. Other UI text read: credit counter 10380, Retry (20), Upgrade, DCC Bridge, Topology Triangle with face/vertex counts (~815k / ~325k), 3D Print, Share, Export, Assets/Property panels with an 'Upload 3D Model' tile and other model thumbnails (gold figures, blue crystalline figure, buildings, pink figure). No export dialog, subscription page or entitlement message appears. Footage is a historical third-party tool reference and says nothing about the current build.

**Skeptic overall:** V09 is a ~65 s iPad Safari screen recording of the Tripo Studio Animate tab with the PURPLE MAH variant; the reviewer's UI readings (tool text, credit counter, motion thumbnails, selection order cast_a_spell -> agree -> angry_02 -> hit_by_head, readouts) hold up against the frames. Twelve of seventeen entries are confirmed; two are corrected (the 2-10 s stretch is not a series of crouches - 2 s is an upright rear stance with two distinct legs; at 30 s one arm is extended, not both bent, and the lower body is thighs into a dark block) and three are overclaimed (12 s 'bent forward', 22 s 'lifting off the base', 56 s arch direction and 'trailing leg', 64 s 'idle/near end' with no playback controls visible). Missed items: the touch indicator and framing shifts showing manual tool orbits, the dotted skeleton overlay, per-clip durations, the purple emissive glow on the fused base (purple-variant only, not gold Athlete_M), the absent playback bar at the static ending, a flat grey viewport with no world background, and no glitch-into-pieces anywhere in the opened frames. Nothing here establishes any export or subscription entitlement, and nothing here describes the current game build. Inspection was by the three contact sheets plus 18 individual 640x480 frames, not a full-rate watch. (sheets re-read: 21)

**Limitations:** 2 s sampling cannot show events shorter than ~2 s (taps, thumbnail hover, brief dialogs, transitions between clips); no audio; exact touch input unknown; the 682×512 decode makes small digits (time readouts, face/vertex counts, motion label spelling) only approximately readable; whether any thumbnail was tapped between 30 and 36 s, 42 and 48 s, or after 60 s is not observable; leg topology (fused vs separate) cannot be resolved from the preview renders; the 'Basic'/'Interactive' tabs were never opened so the full motion library is not visible; nothing in the footage reflects the current MAHWORLD build.

**Deliberately not assumed:** Did not assume any motion was exported, downloaded or purchased; only selection/preview was seen. · Did not assume the account has a subscription or export entitlement from the visible 'Upgrade' button or credit balance. · Did not assume the purple MAH is the same asset as the gold Athlete_M or any other Tripo showcase variant. · Did not assume the fused lower body in the preview is the intended in-game leg anatomy or a lower-body animation reference. · Did not assume the thin line under the model is the skeleton overlay. · Did not assume the exact spelling 'cast_a_spell' or the exact face/vertex counts and time readouts; all flagged approximate. · Did not assume any acceptance-register row applies; the master register was not read, so register_rows are empty. · Did not infer anything about the current build's camera, controls or character from this footage.

---
## V10 — `CameraRecording-03D0894D-032C-49AA-9BDF-4A9EF44159B4.mp4`

**Coverage:** Opened both contact sheets (24 tiles, 0.0 s to 11.32 s at 0.5 s steps) with the Read tool, then opened eight individual 512x910 frames for anatomy, material and viewer-UI detail. Read frames_V10.json for file/decoded values. Grepped MAHWORLD_UNIFIED_CONVERGENCE_MASTER.md and read the text of rows G25-G59 and EXT-01..EXT-14 (only G27, G32, G33, G34, G35, EXT-10-LEGS, EXT-11-WALK are cited).

**Reviewer summary:** V10 is an 11.37 s handheld phone recording of a monitor showing the Tripo Studio web viewer (tab title 'Free Online AI 3D Model Generator ... \| Tripo AI', 'DCC Bridge' button, info panel Faces 18837 / Vertices 21843, bottom rows with render presets and '3D Print / Share / Export'). It shows the PURPLE Tripo character variant, static, while the viewer camera orbits: front (0.0-1.5 s), left profile (2.0-2.5 s), rear three-quarter close-ups of head, back and hips (3.0-6.5 s), right profile (7.0 s) and front again (7.5-11.32 s). Appearance as presented: saturated violet/purple high-gloss metallic body; silver/dark-chrome head, neck, arms, hands and shin panels; pink faceted forehead gem; small pointed ear/horn protrusion and ear cuff on the skull; bracelet rings at the wrists. Anatomy as presented: heavily muscled feminine silhouette with large pectoral masses, cut abs, very narrow waist, wide hips and large glutes; a thin dark band runs across the lower back and between the glutes. The lower body is a fused single limb: two thigh volumes merge with no gap, a dark band circles the limb at knee height, the shin is chrome-panelled and the whole limb tapers to a sharp point with a pink emissive glow; there are no feet and no leg separation anywhere in the clip. This is a historical third-party appearance reference for the purple variant only; it is not the orange-gold target look and says nothing about the current build.

**Skeptic overall:** The colleague's review of V10 is largely accurate: 17 of 23 entries are CONFIRMED against the pixels, 4 are minor OVERCLAIMS (entry 6 describes a knee-bend and tapering limb that f_V10_2p5 does not contain; entry 7 names a 'Resources' label that is not legible at 3.0s; entry 14 asserts an unverifiable 'strongest highlights of the clip' superlative; entry 17 says the Export button is visible at 10.0s when it is clipped until 10.5s), and entry 22 is NOT_VISIBLE because its cited frame cuts off above the knee. No entry misidentifies the content. The clip is a handheld phone recording of the Tripo Studio web viewer (studio.tripo3d.ai, Faces 18837 / Vertices 21843) showing the PURPLE metallic variant with chrome head/neck/arms, a glowing forehead gem, a fused tapered lower body with a knee band and an emissive pink point, orbited front-side-rear-side-front in a static pose. It contains no game world, HUD, gameplay camera or split state, so it cannot support any statement about the current build; it serves only as fused-anatomy and material reference, and the colour is not the owner's orange-gold target. Missed items worth adding: the purple deltoid caps above the chrome arm boundary, the emissive bloom on gem and tip, the diagonal seam lines on hips/thighs and the two-lobed calf below the knee band (relevant to a faithful split derivation), and a caveat that the ~315px frames limit fine-detail claims. (sheets re-read: 16)

**Limitations:** 0.5 s sampling cannot show sub-0.5 s events; there is no audio; exact orbit inputs are unknown. The source is a phone filming a monitor, so moire, glare, perspective skew and blur reduce material and topology fidelity; fine surface detail, exact hue and true specular response cannot be measured from it. UI text is partially truncated (topology value, second face/vertex numbers). The model never animates, so nothing about motion, split legs, walk or transitions can be assessed. This footage cannot show the current build or the orange-gold variant.

**Deliberately not assumed:** Did not assume this purple variant is the owner's colour target; the owner's stated reference is the orange-gold metallic Tripo turntable, a different variant. · Did not assume the character shown is the exact asset currently in the game or that its face/vertex counts match the runtime asset. · Did not assume the dark hip band is clothing or a separate shorts pelvis; only described it as a band on the sculpt. · Did not assume the number or placement of knee joints inside the fused limb. · Did not treat the viewer orbit as any evidence of game camera behaviour. · Did not treat this clip as certification or failure of any split/fuse, walk or anatomy acceptance row; it is a static reference only. · Did not infer material parameters (roughness/metalness values) from a phone capture of a monitor. · Did not conflate this purple showcase with the other two Tripo showcase variants.

---
## V11 — `ScreenRecording_09-10-2026 20-21-24_1.mp4`

**Coverage:** Opened both contact sheets (15 tiles at 0.25 s spacing, 0.00-3.50 s) with the Read tool, then opened three individual full-size frames (0.0 s, 1.5 s, 3.5 s) to judge anatomy and colour at 682x512. Also grepped the convergence master and read the text of rows G32 and EXT-10-LEGS.

**Reviewer summary:** V11 is a 3.57 s, 682x512 screen recording dated 2026-09-10 showing a static four-figure character lineup on a plain off-white backdrop: a heavily muscled male-proportioned gold figure (front and back) beside a narrow-waisted, wide-hipped female-proportioned gold figure (front and back). All four share glossy orange-gold metallic musculature, a silver faceted head crest with spiky gold-brown hair, a column of chrome plates down the chest/spine, dark chrome forearms/gloves, and a fused lower body that tapers to a single dark point instead of two feet. Over the clip the whole group slowly turns from a slight three-quarter angle to face-on and drifts from right-of-centre to the left of the frame; poses never change (arms relaxed at sides). No HUD, tool UI, cursor, caption or watermark is visible at any sampled time. This is historical appearance lineage only; it establishes the gold/chrome material target and the fused single-point anatomy from which split legs are meant to derive, and says nothing about the current build.

**Skeptic overall:** Opened both sheets (all 15 tiles, 0.00-3.50 s) and five full frames. The colleague's review is broadly accurate about the four-figure gold lineup, the chrome crest/plates/forearms, the off-white backdrop and the rotate-then-drift motion. Two substantive corrections: the legs are NOT fused from the pelvis — two separate thighs with a visible cleft converge below the knee, the dark band is at shin level and the terminal spike is gold/tan, not black (entries 2 and 3); and the early motion is a 3D group rotation (three-quarter to frontal), so the 'pan/zoom over a still' alternative in entry 7 is not supported. Faces read as pale chrome, not smooth gold. Entry 9 slightly overstates what is unreadable (eyes and fingers are discernible). Nothing here bears on the current build: no HUD, camera, world or glitch evidence exists in this footage, and no detached parts appear in any tile. Register rows left empty in missed entries because the master register was not read in this task. (sheets re-read: 7)

**Limitations:** Sampling is 0.25 s so any event shorter than that (cuts, flashes, momentary UI) would be missed; there is no audio; exact input (mouse drag, scroll, pan) cannot be seen; the 682x512 decode with figures about 200-250 px tall prevents judging facial features, finger geometry, texture resolution or whether plates are geometry vs texture; source application and provenance (Tripo Studio viewer vs image viewer vs slideshow) cannot be determined because no application chrome is visible; only three individual frames (0.0, 1.5, 3.5 s) were opened at full size, the remaining twelve timestamps were judged from contact-sheet tiles.

**Deliberately not assumed:** Did not assume this footage reflects the current build or the current master's protected anatomy; it is an early reference recording. · Did not assume which of the three Tripo showcase variants (if any) this lineup corresponds to. · Did not assume the source application (Tripo Studio, image viewer, slideshow) or that the motion is a true 3D turntable rather than a 2D pan. · Did not assume the female-proportioned figure is part of the Athlete_M deliverable or that the owner wants it in-game. · Did not treat the lineup's motion as any kind of lower-body animation reference; nothing here bears on split-leg motion beyond the fused source silhouette. · Did not assume the fused single-point lower body is a defect; it is presented here as the source species anatomy. · Did not cite any acceptance-register row other than G32 and EXT-10-LEGS, whose text was actually read. · Did not infer scale/height relationships between the figures beyond what is visible in the frame (male figure visibly taller/broader).

---
## V12 — `mahworld_rig_repair_clip.webm`

**Coverage:** Opened all three contact sheets (36 tiles, 0-102.69 s at 3 s spacing) with the Read tool, then opened 16 individual full-size frames (1024x576 source, displayed ~853x480) wherever a tile was too small to judge anatomy, HUD text, menus, flight/landing state or close-up camera framing. Register rows cited were read via grep of the master (lines 882-1004) before citing. No file was edited, built or deployed.

**Reviewer summary:** V12 is a ~103 s historical HUD gameplay / rig-inspection capture at 1024x576, sampled every 3 s on three sheets plus 16 full frames. No chapters, captions or MANUAL LOOK labels are burned in. The gold Athlete is in fused state for the whole clip and is never seen glitching into pieces; close front/side/back inspections at 42-51 s and 96-102.69 s show a continuous gold-and-dark-chrome mesh, with the gold reading somewhat yellow-olive rather than rich orange-gold. Content: elevated far shot (0 s), close front view (3-6 s), rear walking (9-18 s), flight with LAND button and draining FLIGHT MAHGIC (21-27 s), a crouched gold-ring landing pose (30 s), close mesh inspection (42-51 s), a front-facing lateral-raise/overhead arm sequence with clean hand endpoints (57-63 s), clean centred rear follow toward a FIELD DUMMY (66-78 s), the PHYSICAL move list (Horizontal Push / Vertical Pull / Hinge / Rotation bound to J+swipe) at 81 s and the SPECIAL MAHGIC list (Vector Shot, Trackburst) at 90 s, then close rear/side inspection to the end. The HUD uses an older scheme (ATTACK J, DASH Q, FLY F, no 1-4 row, no H fuse prompt), which differs from the owner's standing control rules. Camera is rear-follow for most gameplay stretches but is in front of or beside the player at 3-6, 27, 42-48, 54-63 and 102 s; from stills it cannot be determined whether those were manual drags.

**Skeptic overall:** The colleague's HUD/text readings, appearance notes and world description hold up against the pixels (entries 0, 1, 2, 4, 5, 6, 9, 12, 14, 16-22 confirmed). The camera-angle claims do not: five entries were wrong about where the camera is. 9 s and 18 s are side profiles, 33 s is a front view, 51 s is a close FRONT view (not the back/glutes), 72 s and 78 s are front views during the supposedly steady rear FIELD DUMMY stretch, and the 42-51 s stretch contains no rear view at all. The 63 s pose is a bent-elbow fists-beside-head pose, not straight overhead. The 30 s 'landing-style pose' is speculation (equally a dash trail). Net picture from this historical capture: the camera is behind the player in only about half the samples (12, 15, 24, 36, 39, 66, 69, 75, 81-99) and is in front or at the side for the rest, with front/rear alternating every few seconds between 54 and 81 s; no burned-in MANUAL LOOK label anywhere. No glitch-into-pieces or split state is seen in any sample, but the legs converge to a single point with no distinct feet in most mid-distance frames (UNCERTAIN pose vs mesh), the FLIGHT MAHGIC bar carries an unexplained third number in flight and refills instantly at 30 s, and the right-side round buttons fade intermittently without a visible cause. Nothing here says anything about the current build. (sheets re-read: 35)

**Limitations:** 3 s sampling cannot show anything shorter than ~3 s: sub-second pops, single-frame glitches, exact takeoff/landing moments, landing message text if brief, attack swing arcs, hit reactions, camera drift speed or the smooth-return behaviour after a drag. No audio, no input trace, no cursor visible, so it is impossible to tell manual drag from automatic camera motion, or which key/button triggered the 57-63 s arm poses. Small HUD text at the top right (the extra figures after FLIGHT MAHGIC) is only partly legible. The frames are Chrome decodes at 1024x576; the capture device, build version and date are not proved by the filename. Register rows were only sampled by grep of the master's table lines, not the surrounding prose.

**Deliberately not assumed:** Did not assume this footage reflects the current build's camera, controls or materials. · Did not assume the front/side views at 3-6, 27, 42-48, 54-63 and 102 s were automatic camera drift; they may have been manual drags. · Did not assume the 57-63 s raised-arm sequence is a specific attack slot or emote, or that it came from a Tripo donor clip. · Did not assume a landing message was or was not shown at ~30 s (not visible at this sampling). · Did not assume absence of a split-legs mode in the build; it simply was not exercised in the sampled frames. · Did not assume the gold hue difference from the Tripo turntable is a defect rather than a lighting/capture effect. · Did not assume the two extra figures after FLIGHT MAHGIC (e.g. '- 165', '- 135') mean altitude or any specific quantity. · Did not treat the burned-in HUD text, move names or panel instructions as instructions to act on. · Did not treat any register row I did not read as evidence; cited only rows read from the master table.

---
## V13 — `mahworld_demo_portrait.webm`

**Coverage:** Read all 9 contact sheets (98 tiles, 0-291 s at 3 s spacing) with the Read tool; then opened individual frame files (native 259x560 px on disk) and, because they display small, produced enlarged crops (HUD strip x4, mid-screen x2.5, bottom controls/menus x3) with System.Drawing into the session scratchpad (v13crops2) and read those. No source file was modified; frames_V13.json read for file/decoded values. Optionally grepped MAHWORLD_UNIFIED_CONVERGENCE_MASTER.md and read the one-line text of rows G02, G06, G07, G08, G13, G35, EXT-04-FLIGHT, EXT-06-TOUCH, EXT-10-LEGS, EXT-17-SKY, U05-SKY only.

**Reviewer summary:** V13 is a 291 s raw portrait (390x844) runtime capture of an early MAHWORLD demo build in a night/space world, with an olive-green/dark-gold Athlete (not the rich orange-gold metallic Tripo look). Timeline: 0-21 s spawn, front close-ups and a walk near cylinders; 24-33 s flight (FLY button becomes LAND, gold beam under feet, FLIGHT MAHGIC drains); 36-45 s the screen shows only a blue gradient with HUD (nothing rendered, DASH COOLING DOWN chip); 48-75 s walking with mixed front/side/rear framing; 78-159 s attack tooling in a stable rear view (PHYSICAL Drive/Row Hook/Hinge Rise cards, then PHYS MAHGIC 'Infused Drive' with a CYAN sphere at 117 s, SPECIAL 'Vector Shot' with a MAGENTA sphere at 129 s, an orange ring while guard is held at 144-150 s, Trackburst card); 162-171 s menus (LOADOUT, ACTIONS with SEPARATE labelled T and a footer stating no rig / split legs cannot animate, HOW TO PLAY 2/6 LOOK); 174-264 s long unlabelled front/side inspection stretches with emote wheel and emote poses; 270 s extreme torso close-up; 276-291 s blue and red recoloured companions beside the player with a hand-raise toward the red one. Not seen: split legs, any glitch-into-pieces, any landing message, any MANUAL LOOK or chapter label. Historical failures vs. current rules: cyan/magenta effects (gold-only rule), SEPARATE on T, night world instead of daytime, prolonged unlabelled front views and a ~9 s empty viewport, HUD layout without the numbered 1-4 attack row. None of this describes the current build.

**Skeptic overall:** Read all nine V13 contact sheets and 37 individual frames (plus upscaled crops of the 168 s menu and of the lower body at 183-216 s, built in the scratchpad). Most HUD/menu/text entries are CONFIRMED. The main correction: the review's claim that the split-leg form never appears is wrong. From 183 s to 213 s the character has two separate legs and two gold feet (clearest at f_V13_204.png, f_V13_195.png), while the fused single-taper form is shown 3-159 s and 216-291 s; the switches fall in the 180-183 s and 213-216 s gaps with no visible HUD feedback. The blue (276 s) and red (285-291 s) same-build figures also have split legs. Other corrections: 57 s is a front three-quarter view, not three-quarter rear; the flight stretch 24-33 s is front-facing (missed); the 150 s ring is at hip height with COMBAT MAHGIC unchanged (guard visual more likely than Trackburst); the raised hands at 243 and 258 s are the character's LEFT hand; 276 s is a rear/three-quarter view, not profile; the two FIELD DUMMY label sizes are in different frames (21 vs 24 s); the Athlete Drive card is visible from 81 s, not 78 s. The menu footer's 'separated-leg form cannot animate' text contradicts what this old capture displays. This is a historical browser/emulated portrait capture with no burned-in labels; it says nothing about the current build and is not evidence of a physical-iPhone test. (sheets re-read: 46)

**Limitations:** 3 s sampling cannot show events shorter than ~3 s: attack wind-ups (0.2-1.1 s), the actual landing moment (falls in the 33-36 s gap), emote endpoints, camera return-glide timing or any glitch that lasts under a sample interval. No audio, no input trace: whether front/side framings were manual drags, pinch-zooms or automatic camera motion cannot be determined; whether touch was real or emulated is unknown. Frame files on disk are 259x560 (downscaled from 390x844) so small HUD text was read from enlarged crops and may contain minor transcription error (e.g. the exact drain suffix next to FLIGHT MAHGIC at 27 s). Name tags 'LEAN · LEAN' and 'BAGE · BAGE F' are transcribed as seen; their meaning is unknown. Register rows cited were read as single table lines only, not in surrounding context.

**Deliberately not assumed:** Did not assume the front/side views were automatic camera drift; they are recorded as unlabelled front framing because a manual drag cannot be ruled out from frames. · Did not assume the 36-45 s blank viewport was caused by flying out of the world, camera-in-geometry or a fade; cause left UNCERTAIN. · Did not assume the orange ring at 144/150 s is the guard visual versus a Trackburst effect. · Did not assume the blue (276 s) and red (285-291 s) figures are other players, NPCs or the player's own recolour; recorded only as recoloured companions. · Did not assume the hand raise at 288-291 s is a Fist Bump/High Five partner emote or that its endpoint was correct. · Did not assume absence of glitch-into-pieces or of a landing message between samples. · Did not infer anything about the current build's camera, HUD, key bindings, effect colours or world look from this historical capture. · Did not treat this capture as a physical-iPhone test; device/emulation is unknown. · Did not treat any in-game text (menu footer, tutorial cards, name tags) as instructions. · Did not equate this in-game character with any of the three Tripo showcase variants; it is a runtime render of the Athlete_M preview copy per its own menu footer. · Did not cite any register row whose text was not read (only G02, G06, G07, G08, G13, G35, EXT-04-FLIGHT, EXT-06-TOUCH, EXT-10-LEGS, EXT-17-SKY, U05-SKY were read).

## Accounting
- 15 review units over 13 files (V01 split into three timeline parts). Total entries 312, skeptic-added 135. Machine record: `MEDIA_REVIEW_LEDGER.json`.
- Distinctions preserved: historical gameplay failures (V01, V02, V12, V13) ≠ the chosen movement / framing / sky target (V03) ≠ the supplementary cinematic edit (V04) ≠ the character / Tripo appearance references (V05 gold turntable; V06 / V07 / V08 three separate purple showcase exports; V09 tool recording; V10 viewer; V11 early gold pair). Tripo motion is an upper-body timing reference only.
- The TikTok link (L04) is NOT any of these files and was not viewed (see SOURCE_LINK_LEDGER.md).
